Motivation: 现有多参数化神经量子态中，自回归模型虽能实现精确独立采样，但其优化方法存在不足：Adam 可扩展但忽略函数空间几何，随机重配置方法虽理论完备但计算昂贵且数值不稳定。为填补这一空白，需要一种兼具可扩展性与理论保证的 NQS 优化算法。

Method: 将变分能量最小化重新表述为基于 Born 分布的 Advantage Policy-Gradient 问题，引入信任域优化思想。提出 PWO 算法，在振幅通道裁剪概率比变化，在相位通道裁剪相位增量，避免显式矩阵求逆，并支持样本在多轮更新中复用。

Result: 在 Ising 和阻挫 J₁-J₂ 一维/二维自旋系统中，PWO 在稳定性和壁钟收敛速度上均优于 Adam、minSR 和 SPRING。成功将 15 亿参数的 RWKV-7 模型作为 NQS 进行微调，验证了该方法在远超以往工作（三个数量级以上）的规模上的有效性。

Conclusion: PWO 是一种可扩展、稳定且具有理论保证的神经量子态优化算法，填补了自回归 NQS 优化方法的空白，并展示了在超大模型上进行量子变分优化的可行性。

Abstract: Neural quantum states (NQS) provide a flexible and scalable framework for approximating quantum many-body wavefunctions. Among NQS parameterizations, autoregressive models are especially attractive because they enable exact, independent sampling from the Born distribution, avoiding the autocorrelation and mixing issues of Markov chain methods. Yet their optimization remains comparatively underexplored: Adam is a scalable method but ignores function space geometry, while stochastic reconfiguration is principled but costly and numerically fragile in large models. To address this gap, we show that variational energy minimization can be viewed as an advantage policy-gradient problem over the Born distribution, motivating trust-region optimization for NQS training. We introduce Proximal Wavefunction Optimization (PWO), a principled trust-region algorithm that clips probability-ratio changes in the amplitude channel and phase increments in the phase channel. PWO avoids explicit matrix inversion, reuses samples across multiple updates, and combines the scalability of first-order optimization with theoretical guarantees. Across Ising and frustrated $J_1$-$J_2$ one- and two-dimensional spin systems, PWO improves stability and wall-clock convergence over Adam, minSR, and SPRING. Finally, we fine-tune a $1.5$B-parameter RWKV-7 model, demonstrating NQS optimization at a scale over three orders of magnitude beyond prior work.

</details>


### [149] [Self-Gating Attention for Efficient Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02344)
*Dezheng Wang,Tong Chen,Wei Yuan,Congyan Chen,Shihua Li,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Gating Attention (SGA)，一种替代标准自注意力机制的轻量级注意力机制，通过共享可学习矩阵和输入相关残差组件表示注意力分数，将时间复杂度和内存复杂度从二次降低至线性，在多个时序预测基准上保持竞争性能的同时提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自注意力在时序预测中具有二次时间和内存复杂度（相对于回溯长度），限制了其在资源受限或高吞吐量预测系统中的应用。作者观察到时序预测中自注意力图存在跨时间步的冗余模式，这源于真实世界时间序列的重复时间模式和相对稳定的时间相关性，为设计更高效的注意力机制提供了契机。

Method: 提出Self-Gating Attention (SGA)，一种即插即用的注意力机制。其注意力分数由一个共享可学习矩阵（捕获通用注意力模式）和一个输入相关残差组件（捕获输入特异性变化）构成。SGA避免了标准注意力中查询和键的投影计算，实现了时间和得分矩阵内存复杂度均为O(L)（L为回溯长度）。

Result: 将SGA集成到多个预测主干网络中，在涵盖电力、金融、天气、医疗监测、人类活动和气候记录等9个真实世界数据集上，与标准自注意力和轻量级注意力变体进行对比。结果表明SGA在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，显著提升了推理效率。

Conclusion: SGA作为一种高效且性能有竞争力的注意力替代方案，为时序预测中资源受限或高吞吐量的部署场景提供了可行选择。基准测试结果为面向部署的实用性提供了证据支持。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have shown strong potential in time series forecasting, where multi-head self-attention is widely used to capture temporal dependencies across historical timestamps. However, standard self-attention has quadratic time and memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. This cost may limit its use in resource-constrained or high-throughput forecasting systems, where fast and memory-efficient inference is important. Through qualitative and quantitative analyses, we observe that self-attention maps in time series forecasting often contain redundant patterns across different timestamps. This phenomenon can be related to the repeated temporal patterns and relatively stable temporal correlations in many real-world time series. Motivated by this observation, we propose Self-Gating Attention (SGA), a plug-and-play attention mechanism that represents the attention score with a shared learnable matrix and an input-dependent residual component. The shared matrix captures common attention patterns, while the residual component captures input-dependent variations. In this way, SGA avoids the query and key projections used in standard attention score computation, leading to linear time and score-matrix memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. We integrate SGA into several forecasting backbones and compare it with standard self-attention and lightweight attention variants on nine publicly available real-world datasets covering electricity, finance, weather, medical monitoring, human activity, and climate records. The results show that SGA improves inference efficiency on public benchmarks while maintaining competitive forecasting performance against state-of-the-art attention mechanisms. These benchmark results provide deployment-oriented evidence.

</details>


### [150] [DecompRL: Solving Harder Problems by Learning Modular Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02390)
*Juliette Decugis,Fabian Gloeckle,Francis Bach,Taco Cohen,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DecompRL算法，通过将复杂问题分解为可独立求解的子模块并重组，将GPU token成本降低约50倍，在LLM编码任务上超越标准RL基线，解决了标准生成无法触及的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（重复采样和RL）在基策略生成正确解概率接近零时均会失败，因为搜索空间过大，无论多少采样或梯度信号都无法克服。

Method: 提出DecompRL，一种显式学习分解和实现层次化代码结构的RL算法。将问题分解为多个独立可解的子模块，重组k个模块的n个实现可产生k^n个候选解，将瓶颈从GPU推理转移到廉价的CPU评估。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests上（Qwen 2.5 7B, Code World Model 32B），DecompRL在每问题超过10^5 token时优于标准和多样性优化RL基线，GPU token成本降低约50倍，解决了标准生成无法解决的问题。

Conclusion: 与其"更努力地采样"，不如"让任务更简单"——通过分解问题为独立子模块并重组，可以显著提升LLM解决困难问题的能力。

Abstract: How can Large Language Models (LLMs) solve problems they currently cannot? Repeated sampling scales test-time compute but GPU cost grows linearly with attempts, while reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards improves single-attempt accuracy at the expense of sample diversity. Both strategies ultimately fail when the base policy has near-zero probability of producing a correct solution: no amount of sampling or gradient signal can overcome a search space that is simply too large. We take a different approach: rather than sampling harder, we make the task easier by decomposing problems into smaller, independently solvable sub-functions whose implementations can be recombined. Since off-the-shelf models are not trained for this modular generation, we introduce DecompRL, an RL algorithm that explicitly learns to decompose and implement hierarchical code structures. Recombining $k$ implementations of $n$ modules yields up to $k^{n}$ candidate solutions, shifting the bottleneck from GPU inference to cheap CPU evaluation and cutting GPU token cost by $\sim$50$\times$. On LiveCodeBench and CodeContests (Qwen~2.5~7B, Code World Model~32B), DecompRL outperforms standard and diversity-optimized RL baselines beyond $10^5$ tokens per problem, solving problems that standard generation cannot reach.

</details>


### [151] [Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02423)
*Zhuowei Chen,Liwei Chen,Christian Schunn,Raquel Coelho,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuFS提出了一种基于神经元激活模式的主动少样本学习框架，通过模型内部动态而非输出级信号来选择最有价值的标注样本，在推理和文本分类任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动少样本学习方法依赖输出级信号（如预测熵、语义相似度）进行样本选择，忽略了能够揭示模型知识空洞的内部动态信息。

Method: NeuFS框架包含：(1) 利用神经元激活模式直接表示样本；(2) 双准则选择策略：基于神经元模式的多样性选择确保样本覆盖面，以及通过量化神经元共识识别模型易产生幻觉的信息性、挑战性样本。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，NeuFS在推理和文本分类任务中均优于现有AFSL基线方法。消融实验验证了内部神经元激活作为选择信号比外部嵌入更有效。

Conclusion: 基于模型内部神经元动态的样本选择是一种比输出级代理信号更原则性且有效的主动少样本学习方法。

Abstract: Active Few-Shot Learning (AFSL) adapts LLMs to specialized domains by identifying the most valuable unlabeled samples for annotation and use as few-shot demonstrations, effectively reducing human annotation costs while promoting high performance. However, existing methods typically rely on output-level signals for sample identification, such as predictive entropy or semantic similarities with test-time data based on external embeddings, which often overlook models' internal dynamics, which could pinpoint specific knowledge gaps. To bridge this gap, we propose NeuFS, a Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning framework that shifts the selection paradigm from output-level proxies to models' internal dynamics. NeuFS utilizes neuron activation patterns to represent sample directly, and includes a dual-criteria selection strategy that: (1) ensures few-shot sample diversity with neuron patterns for broader example coverage, while (2) prioritizing on identifying informative and challenging few-shot samples LLMs tend to hallucinate by quantifying neuron consensus. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that NeuFS excels in both reasoning and text classification tasks, outperforming existing AFSL baselines. Ablation studies further highlight that internal neuron activations provide a more principled and effective selection signal than external embeddings, validating the superiority of the proposed NeuFS.

</details>


### [152] [QFedAgent: Quantum-Enhanced Personalized Federated Learning for Multi-Agent Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02426)
*Quoc Bao Phan,Tuy Tan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QFedAgent，一种混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架，用于多智能体活动识别，通过变分量子电路融合模块（仅72个量子旋转参数 vs. 经典融合的33K参数）实现约10倍参数缩减，在OPPORTUNITY数据集上达到97.7%的平均测试准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在多智能体机器人感知应用中面临多模态传感器数据的非独立同分布（non-IID）问题，且经典融合模块参数开销和通信成本过高，亟需一种参数高效的个性化联邦学习方案。

Method: 提出QFedAgent混合量子-经典框架，采用变分量子电路（VQC）作为融合模块，通过量子态编码和纠缠机制建模加速度计-陀螺仪交互，仅需72个量子旋转参数；结合个性化联邦学习策略处理subject-based non-IID数据分布。

Result: 在OPPORTUNITY数据集上，subject-based non-IID划分下达到97.7%平均测试准确率；参数总量相比经典多层感知机融合减少约10倍（72 vs. 33K）。

Conclusion: 参数高效的量子融合在联邦学习多模态感知任务中能够与经典联邦基线方法保持竞争力，验证了量子-经典混合架构在资源受限场景下的可行性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive robotic sensing applications. However, multi-agent systems generate heterogeneous and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) multimodal sensor streams that degrade conventional FL algorithms, while classical fusion modules introduce substantial parameter overhead and communication cost. This paper proposes QFedAgent, a hybrid quantum-classical personalized FL framework for multi-agent activity recognition. The approach integrates a variational quantum circuit fusion module that models accelerometer--gyroscope interactions through quantum state encoding and entanglement, requiring only 72 quantum rotation parameters versus 33K in classical multi-layer perceptron-based fusion, achieving approximately 10x total parameter reduction. Experiments on the OPPORTUNITY dataset under subject-based non-IID partitions demonstrate 97.7% mean test accuracy, confirming that parameter-efficient quantum fusion remains competitive with conventional federated baselines.

</details>


### [153] [Extreme Adaptive Transformer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02437)
*Sanjeev Shrestha,Hui Liu,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出极端自适应Transformer（Exformer），通过引入包含Local、Stride和Extreme三个稀疏组件的极端自适应注意力机制，显式建模正常与极端事件之间的时序依赖关系，在四个真实水文流量数据集上实现了优于现有方法的3天预报性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测中罕见但关键的极端事件（如洪水峰值）通常被现有Transformer模型均匀对待，导致极端模式被低估。水文预报中流量分布高度偏斜，极端峰值对洪水监测、水资源管理和预警系统至关重要，亟需能够显式建模极端事件依赖关系的预测框架。

Method: 提出Exformer框架，核心是极端自适应注意力机制，包含三个稀疏组件：Local组件捕获短期时序依赖，Stride组件捕获周期依赖，Extreme组件选择性建模正常与极端流模式之间的事件感知依赖。

Result: 在四个真实水文流量数据集上，Exformer在3天预测任务中优于所有基线模型，证明了极端感知注意力机制能有效提升Transformer在不平衡时间序列（含罕见但重要事件）上的预测能力。

Conclusion: 显式融入极端感知注意力能够显著提升Transformer模型在具有罕见但关键事件的非均衡时间序列上的预测性能，为水文极端事件预报提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Time series forecasting remains challenging when the underlying data contain rare but critical extreme events. This issue is particularly important in hydrologic forecasting, where streamflow distributions are often highly skewed and extreme peaks can have substantial impacts on flood monitoring, water resource management, and early warning systems. Although Transformer-based forecasting models have achieved strong performance by modeling long-range temporal dependencies, they typically treat all time points uniformly and may therefore underrepresent rare extreme patterns. In this paper, we propose the Extreme-Adaptive Transformer (Exformer), a forecasting framework designed to explicitly model temporal dependencies involving both normal and extreme events. Exformer introduces an extreme-adaptive attention mechanism composed of three sparse components: Local, Stride, and Extreme. The Local and Stride components capture short-term and periodic temporal dependencies, respectively, while the Extreme component selectively models event-aware dependencies between normal and extreme streamflow patterns. Experiments on four real-world hydrologic streamflow datasets show that Exformer achieves superior 3-day forecasting performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines. Our findings demonstrate that explicitly incorporating extreme-aware attention improves the forecasting capacity of Transformer models on imbalanced time series with rare but consequential events.

</details>


### [154] [Understanding the Robustness of Distributed Self-Supervised Learning Frameworks Against Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02447)
*Xuanyu Chen,Nan Yang,Shuai Wang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）在非IID数据下的鲁棒性进行了严格理论分析，发现掩码图像建模（MIM）比对比学习（CL）对异构数据更鲁棒，且联邦学习（FL）鲁棒性不逊于去中心化学习（DecL）。基于理论提出MAR损失函数，实验验证了理论发现及MAR损失的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式自监督学习面临数据异构性的关键挑战，但现有工作缺乏对不同D-SSL框架如何应对这一挑战的理论理解。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用严格理论分析研究D-SSL框架在非IID设置下的鲁棒性，并基于理论洞察提出MAR损失（MIM目标结合局部到全局对齐正则化），通过跨模型架构和分布式设置的实验验证。

Result: 理论结果表明：MIM预训练比CL对异构数据更鲁棒；D-SSL鲁棒性随平均网络连通性增加而提升，FL不比DecL鲁棒性差。实验验证了理论并确认MAR损失的有效性。

Conclusion: 为未来D-SSL算法设计提供了坚实的理论基础，并通过MAR损失展示了理论的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Recent research has introduced distributed self-supervised learning (D-SSL) approaches to leverage vast amounts of unlabeled decentralized data. However, D-SSL faces the critical challenge of data heterogeneity, and there is limited theoretical understanding of how different D-SSL frameworks respond to this challenge. To fill this gap, we present a rigorous theoretical analysis of the robustness of D-SSL frameworks under non-IID (non-independent and identically distributed) settings. Our results show that pre-training with Masked Image Modeling (MIM) is inherently more robust to heterogeneous data than Contrastive Learning (CL), and that the robustness of decentralized SSL increases with average network connectivity, implying that federated learning (FL) is no less robust than decentralized learning (DecL). These findings provide a solid theoretical foundation for guiding the design of future D-SSL algorithms. To further illustrate the practical implications of our theory, we introduce MAR loss, a refinement of the MIM objective with local-to-global alignment regularization. Extensive experiments across model architectures and distributed settings validate our theoretical insights, and additionally confirm the effectiveness of MAR loss as an application of our analysis.

</details>


### [155] [Neuron-Aware Data Selection for Annotation-Free LLM Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02460)
*Zhuowei Chen,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Neuron-OPSD，一种利用神经元激活指导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建的无标注自蒸馏框架，通过在线策略蒸馏提升专业领域任务性能，同时保持跨域泛化并避免校准误差膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无标注自演化方法（如SFT/GRPO变体、基于奖励的在线策略RL）存在域外性能下降或校准误差膨胀的问题，且专业领域专家标注成本高昂，缺乏真实交互反馈或人工标注监督。

Method: 提出Neuron-OPSD框架：利用内部神经元激活指导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建，通过在线策略蒸馏从教师分布中训练模型，全程无需真实标签。

Result: 在专业领域基准上，Neuron-OPSD提升了域内任务性能，保持了跨域泛化能力，并缓解了校准崩溃问题，优于现有无标注基线方法。

Conclusion: Neuron-OPSD适用于在线交互或外部监督成本高昂的场景，与依赖已标注奖励轨迹的离线RL方法在概念上不同，是一种数据驱动的无标注自蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) without real-world interaction feedback or human-labeled supervision remains challenging, particularly in specialized domains where expert annotations are costly to obtain. Recent annotation-free self-evolution methods address this by using the model's own outputs as supervision signals, constructing a teacher via additional context and aggregating predictions across multiple rollouts through majority voting to produce pseudo-labels. However, these approaches are not without drawbacks: SFT- and GRPO-based variants suffer out-of-domain performance degradation, while reward-based on-policy RL inflates calibration error. In this paper, we propose Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation (Neuron-OPSD), a data-centric framework for annotation-free self-distillation that leverages internal neuron activations to guide both training-data selection and teacher context construction. The model is then trained via on-policy distillation from the teacher distribution, requiring no ground-truth labels at any stage. Across specialized-domain benchmarks, Neuron-OPSD improves in-domain task performance while preserving cross-domain generalization and mitigating calibration collapse over prior annotation-free baselines. This framework is particularly relevant to settings where online interaction or external supervision is costly or infeasible, and is conceptually distinct from offline RL approaches that rely on logged, reward-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond Adam: SOAP and Muon for Faster, Label-Efficient Training of Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02499)
*Gil Harari,Yoel Zimmermann,Ola Tangen Kulseng,Laura Zichi,Chuin Wei Tan,Marc L. Descoteaux,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Muon、SOAP和SOAP-Muon等矩阵结构化优化器与Adam在训练NequIP和Allegro MLIP模型上的表现，发现这些新优化器在收敛速度和最终精度上显著优于Adam，其中SOAP和SOAP-Muon表现最为稳健，且改进在部分力监督场景下尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIP训练中优化器的选择长期被忽视，社区默认使用Adam及其变体，缺乏对更优优化器的系统探索。

Method: 在NequIP和Allegro两种MLIP架构上，实现了Muon、SOAP和SOAP-Muon三种矩阵结构化优化器，并与Adam进行系统比较。

Result: 新优化器在收敛速度和最终精度上均显著优于Adam；SOAP和SOAP-Muon表现稳健且一致优于Adam，而Muon仅带来部分提升；改进在部分力监督条件下尤为明显。

Conclusion: 优化器选择是MLIP训练中一个被忽视但影响显著的设计维度，SOAP和SOAP-Muon可作为Adam的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have become a hallmark of AI for scientific simulation. While efforts on new architectures and datasets have led to increasingly accurate and general models, the choice of optimizer for training has largely remained unexplored, defaulting to Adam and its variants in the community. Here, we implement and systematically compare a class of recently proposed matrix-structured optimizers, including Muon, SOAP, and the hybrid SOAP-Muon, for training NequIP and Allegro MLIP models. We find that these optimizers can substantially outperform Adam in both convergence speed and final accuracy. SOAP and SOAP-Muon emerge as robust and consistently strong methods, while Muon only provides partial gains relative to Adam. The improvements are particularly pronounced under partial force supervision. Our results indicate that optimizer choice is an overlooked yet impactful design axis for MLIPs.

</details>


### [157] [DemoPSD: Disagreement-Modulated Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02502)
*Yunhe Li,Hao Shi,Wenhao Liu,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Zhongxiang Dai,Shuang Qiu,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DemoPSD通过选择性采用教师引导，基于反向KL重心目标在教师分布与学生分布之间动态平衡，有效解决了自蒸馏中的特权信息泄露和探索能力抑制问题，在科学推理任务上超越GRPO和SDPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD中教师基于特权信息进行密集token级监督会导致过拟合、抑制探索、跨域泛化差，以及更根本的特权信息泄露问题——学生编码了测试时不可用的答案依赖捷径。

Method: 提出DemoPSD框架，核心是选择性采用教师引导：构建反向KL重心目标（教师分布与学生分布的加权几何组合），利用两分布间的差异动态控制每个token位置的混合比例，在跟随教师与保持自身推理能力之间取得平衡。

Result: 在SciKnowEval四个科学领域上，DemoPSD优于GRPO和SDPO；维持更高训练熵；鲁棒地泛化到OOD的GPQA基准；理论上证明了泄露衰减和探索保持两个性质。

Conclusion: DemoPSD通过自适应平衡教师与学生分布，有效缓解了自蒸馏中的特权信息泄露并保持了探索能力，为LLM推理训练提供了更稳健的框架。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a practical method for training large language models (LLMs) to reason, where a single model acts as both the teacher and the student with different levels of information access. However, recent studies have found that the teacher's dense token-level supervision, conditioned on privileged information, can lead to overfitting to in-domain patterns, suppress exploration, and hurt cross-domain generalization, while also introducing a more fundamental issue: *privileged information leakage*, where the student encodes answer-dependent shortcuts that are unavailable at test time. We introduce **DemoPSD**, a novel framework that resolves such problems through the idea of *selective adoption of teacher guidance*. Instead of fitting the full teacher distribution, DemoPSD steers the student toward a *reverse-KL barycenter target*, a weighted geometric combination of the teacher and student distributions, that naturally balances learning from the teacher with preserving the student's own reasoning capacity. We measure the difference between their distributions and use such a discrepancy to adaptively control the blending at each token position. We provably show that DemoPSD achieves **(1)** *leakage attenuation*, i.e., effective mitigation of privileged information leakage; and **(2)** *exploration preservation*, i.e., preservation of exploration capacity under dense token-level distillation. Extensive experiments on SciKnowEval across four scientific fields show that DemoPSD outperforms both GRPO and SDPO while maintaining higher training entropy and robustly generalizing to out-of-distribution GPQA benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Field-Deployable RF Capture System for Indoor, Outdoor, and Foliage Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01368)
*Lawrence Obiuwevwi,Krzysztof J. Rechowicz,Vikas Ashok,Sachin Shetty,Peter B. Foytik,Jared Cochran,Jeff Bobrow,Sampath Jayarathna*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于HackRF One、Raspberry Pi 5、GNSS接收机和电池供电的便携式射频捕获系统，可连续记录20 Msps的IQ数据（SigMF格式），并在三种真实场景（密林、城市户外、室内办公室）的2.45 GHz实验中验证了其性能，展示了多径、干扰和建筑穿透损耗等不同传播特征，系统写吞吐量达75-85 MB/s且无丢样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RF测量平台多为实验室级、成本高昂或依赖固定基础设施，难以满足快速、分布式或长期现场测量的需求，因此需要一种便携、低成本且可靠的方案。

Method: 构建整合HackRF One SDR、Raspberry Pi 5、GNSS接收器、稳压电池供电和高速固态存储的紧凑系统；以20 Msps率连续记录IQ数据，采用SigMF格式存储并嵌入每段的位置和时间元数据；分别在密林、城市户外和室内办公室三种真实环境进行2.45 GHz现场实验。

Result: 密林场景信号接近底噪（-76至-82 dBFS），谱结构稀疏；城市户外场景呈现30 dB动态范围的多径活动、WiFi信道重叠和ISM频段干扰；室内场景中WiFi信道占主导，建筑穿透损耗约20-25 dB，干扰底噪比室外高8-10 dB。系统持续维持75-85 MB/s写入吞吐量，无丢样或缓冲区欠载，GNSS同步时间低于1秒，定位精度达米级。

Conclusion: 该便携、低成本的SDR平台能够生成高保真、带地理标签的IQ数据集，适用于频谱表征、干扰分析、无线电环境映射及环境感知无线研究。

Abstract: Reliable and reproducible radio-frequency (RF) measurements in real-world environments are essential for characterizing spectrum behavior across unlicensed ISM and WiFi bands, licensed mid-band allocations, and emerging next-generation wireless deployments. Existing measurement platforms are often laboratory-grade, cost-prohibitive, or dependent on fixed infrastructure, limiting their practicality for rapid, distributed, or long-duration field campaigns. This paper presents a compact, battery-powered RF capture system integrating a HackRF One software-defined radio, Raspberry Pi 5, GNSS receiver, regulated battery supply, and high-speed solid-state storage. The platform records continuous IQ data at up to 20 Msps in SigMF format with per-segment location and timing metadata for reproducible spectrum analysis. Field experiments at 2.45 GHz in dense foliage, urban outdoor, and indoor office environments reveal distinct propagation signatures. Foliage measurements remain near the noise floor at -76 to -82 dBFS with limited spectral structure, consistent with strong canopy attenuation. Urban measurements show multipath activity across a 30 dB dynamic range, overlapping WiFi channels, and frequent ISM-band interference. Indoor measurements show dominant WiFi channels, an estimated 20 to 25 dB building entry loss relative to outdoor conditions, and an 8 to 10 dB higher interference floor caused by structural reflections. The system sustained 75 to 85 MB/s write throughput with no dropped samples or buffer underruns, while GNSS synchronization remained below one second with meter-level positioning. These results show that a portable, cost-effective SDR platform can produce high-fidelity, geotagged IQ datasets for spectrum characterization, interference analysis, radio environment mapping, and environment-aware wireless research.

</details>


### [159] [Physically-Aware Preemptive Virtual Channels for Deadlock-Free AXI Networks-on-Chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01430)
*Lorenzo Leone,Luca Colagrande,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对多核SoC中AXI4协议级读写依赖导致的死锁问题，本文比较了多平面NoC和轻量级VC方案，并提出Preemptive VCs架构，相比多平面方案可节省76%链路资源，仅增加3%路由器面积开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多核SoC规模扩展，NoC需支撑高内存带宽且保证无死锁。AXI4系统中读写流量间的协议级依赖会在网络端点造成循环等待，即使路由算法本身无死锁，也需要解耦流量类别，但现有方案（多平面或传统VC）存在链路冗余或控制复杂度高的问题。

Method: 评估四种死锁无关的AXI4流量类别分离方案：多平面基线和三种轻量级VC设计。提出Preemptive VCs，一种物理感知架构，通过优化VC组织降低链路资源需求。

Result: Preemptive VCs相比多平面设计可节省高达76%的链路资源，频率相当，路由器面积开销仅增加3%。

Conclusion: 对于现代宽链路NoC，轻量级VC方案（特别是Preemptive VCs）能有效替代多平面方法，在保证无死锁的同时大幅降低链路成本，面积开销极小。

Abstract: As many-core Systems-on-Chip (SoCs) continue to scale, Networks-on-Chip (NoCs) must sustain increasingly high memory bandwidth while preserving deadlock freedom. In AXI4 systems, protocol-level dependencies between read and write traffic can create circular waits at the network endpoints, even when the routing algorithm itself is deadlock-free. Decoupling these traffic classes avoids such dependencies, but exposes a key implementation trade-off: multiplane NoCs duplicate wide physical links and increase routing pressure, whereas conventional Virtual Channel (VC) routers add substantial control complexity, area, and timing overhead. This work revisits this trade-off for modern wide-link NoCs. We evaluate four deadlock-free AXI4 traffic-class separation schemes: a multiplane baseline and three lightweight VC-based designs. Among these designs, we propose Preemptive VCs, a physically-aware architecture that can save up to 76% of link resources with comparable frequency and only 3% router area overhead relative to the multiplane design.

</details>


### [160] [MxGLUT: A Reconfigurable LUT-Centric Broadcast Dataflow Accelerator for Mixed-Precision GEMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01607)
*Weiyu Zhou,Chen Ding,Mingyuan Liu,Liangyu Gan,Yukun Feng,Hao Jia,Haoming Chu,Lirong Zheng,Ning Ma,Yuxiang Huan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出MxGLUT——一种基于混合精度LUT计算单元和可重构广播数据流的加速器，统一支持FP8-INT4和FP8-FP8的LLM推理，消除冗余FP数据通路，相比FIGLUT基线，面积效率0.492 TFLOPS/mm²，能效11.58 TFLOPS/W，在Llama家族上预填阶段加速比最高2.16×，解码阶段1.49×，能耗降至0.44×和0.71×，困惑度增加不超过1.70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LUT加速器主要面向FP8-INT4计算，仍依赖独立浮点数据通路处理注意力GEMM，导致硬件冗余和非统一的混合精度执行；此外，静态数据流无法适配LLM推理中预填和解码两个阶段的差异需求。

Method: 提出可重构LUT中心广播数据流加速器MxGLUT，核心是混合精度LUT处理单元，在统一LUT计算框架下同时支持FP8-INT4和FP8-FP8 GEMM，无需专用FP乘法器或额外FP数据通路；采用可重构广播数据流，在预填阶段局部化累加操作，在解码阶段利用权重复用。

Result: 在UMC 28nm CMOS 200MHz下，乘法器面积减少56.92%，功耗在FP8-INT4和FP8-FP8模式下分别降低77.07%和78.35%；面积效率0.492 TFLOPS/mm²，能效11.58 TFLOPS/W；相比FIGLUT基线，增加FP8-FP8支持仅牺牲2.57%面积效率和3.34%能效。在Llama家族上，预填和解码阶段延迟加速比最高2.16×和1.49×，能耗分别降至0.44×和0.71×，困惑度增加最多1.70%。

Conclusion: MxGLUT通过统一LUT计算框架和可重构数据流，有效解决了LLM推理中混合精度计算和两阶段数据流适配问题，在面积、功耗和性能上均取得显著优势。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference suffers from growing inefficiency across the prefill and decode phases, especially under weight-only quantization, where activations remain in FP8 while weights are compressed to low-bit integers. Existing LUT-based accelerators mainly target FP8-INT4 computation and still rely on separate floating-point (FP) datapaths for attention GEMM operations, leading to redundant hardware and non-unified mixed-precision execution. Moreover, their static dataflows are poorly matched to the distinct prefill and decode phases. To address these challenges, we propose MxGLUT, a reconfigurable LUT-centric broadcast (RLB) dataflow accelerator built on mixed-precision LUT-based processing elements (MxLPEs). Guided by a unified LUT-based execution framework, MxGLUT organizes both FP8-INT4 and FP8-FP8 GEMMs under a single LUT-based compute mechanism without dedicated FP multipliers or additional FP datapaths, and further adopts the RLB dataflow that localizes heavy partial-sum accumulation during the prefill phase and exploits weight reuse in the decode phase. Synthesized in UMC $28\,\mathrm{nm}$ CMOS at $200~\mathrm{MHz}$, MxGLUT reduces multiplier area by up to $56.92\%$ and power by up to $77.07\%$ and $78.35\%$ in FP8-INT4 and FP8-FP8 modes, respectively. At the accelerator level, MxGLUT achieves an area efficiency of $0.492~\mathrm{TFLOPS/mm^2}$ and an energy efficiency of $11.58~\mathrm{TFLOPS/W}$, while adding native FP8-FP8 support incurs only $2.57\%$ and $3.34\%$ reductions in area and energy efficiency, respectively, relative to the FP8-INT4-only FIGLUT baseline. Across the Llama family, MxGLUT achieves up to $2.16\times$ and $1.49\times$ latency speedup, and reduces normalized energy to $0.44\times$ and $0.71\times$ in prefill and decode, respectively, with at most $1.70\%$ perplexity increase.

</details>


### [161] [3DLS: A 3D Logic-Stacked Architecture for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01617)
*Jaehun Lee,In-Jun Jung,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出3DLS，一种逻辑-on-逻辑3D堆叠芯片架构，通过垂直互连分离KV缓存传输与张量并行集合通信，解决PD分离LLM服务中混合流量竞争问题，相比平面基线实现1.49倍吞吐量提升和60.2%的端到端延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM服务中，PD分离架构与张量并行结合时，KV缓存传输和TP集合通信共享同一横向D2D互连，导致混合流量竞争，增加通信延迟，拉长token生成间隔，降低端到端服务性能。

Method: 提出3DLS逻辑-on-逻辑3D堆叠芯片架构，通过垂直互连路由KV缓存传输，同时在横向D2D互连上保留解码侧TP集合通信，实现流量类别的物理隔离。

Result: 相比共享互连的平面基线，3DLS实现高达1.49倍吞吐量提升和60.2%的端到端延迟降低；相比工作负载感知的优先级管理平面基线，仍实现1.17倍吞吐量提升和31.4%的端到端延迟降低。

Conclusion: 物理隔离是未来基于芯片的PD分离LLM服务系统的重要设计原则。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving increasingly combines prefill-decode (PD) disaggregation with tensor parallelism (TP) to support large models and long contexts. In conventional 2D/2.5D chiplet architectures, layer-wise prefill-to-decode KV-cache transfer decode-side TP collectives share the same lateral die-to-die (D2D) interconnect, creating mixed-traffic contention on the decode critical path. This contention increases communication latency, prolongs token generation intervals, and degrades end-to-end serving performance. We propose 3DLS, a logic-on-logic 3D-stacked chiplet architecture that separates traffic classes by routing KV-cache transfers through vertical interconnects while preserving decode-side TP collectives on the lateral D2D fabric. 3DLS achieves up to 1.49$\times$ throughput and 60.2\% lower end-to-end (E2E) latency over the shared-fabric planar baseline, and still achieves up to 1.17$\times$ throughput and 31.4\% lower E2E latency over a workload-aware priority-managed planar baseline. These results highlight that physical isolation is an important design principle for future chiplet-based PD-disaggregated LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [162] [Approximate Attention Weighting for Sustainable FPGA-Based Vision Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01798)
*Muhammad Usman,Shujaat Khan,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无BRAM的近似注意力权重计算单元，用于在FPGA上高效部署Vision Transformer推理。该单元采用16段分段线性函数在分布式LUT上近似softmax中的自然指数，在Xilinx Zynq-7020上仅消耗1444个LUT、77个DSP且不使用BRAM，top-1准确率与精确softmax参考的绝对差异在0.20%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformer在边缘视觉检测与监控应用中具有巨大潜力，但在小型FPGA上部署面临挑战，因为自注意力中的softmax阶段需要指数运算和归一化，硬件实现成本高昂。现有基于CORDIC流水线或BRAM查找表的方法占用面积大、功耗高。

Method: 设计了一个无BRAM的近似注意力权重单元，采用16段分段线性函数在分布式LUT上近似自然指数，避免使用BRAM查找表。该方法保持自然指数形式，保留了预训练注意力温度，无需针对特定模型重新校准。

Result: 在Xilinx Zynq-7020上实现，完整注意力行核消耗1444个LUT、77个DSP，不使用BRAM。硬件精确仿真显示，与精确softmax参考相比，ViT系列模型top-1准确率绝对差异在0.20%以内。

Conclusion: 该设计证明了在资源受限的边缘AI平台上实现能效型ViT推理的潜力。

Abstract: Vision Transformers have reshaped computer vision by using self-attention to capture global context across image regions. This makes them attractive for edge visual inspection and monitoring in applications such as renewable-energy infrastructure, industrial quality control, medical imaging, and autonomous-system sensing. However, deploying ViTs on small FPGAs remains challenging because the softmax stage in self-attention requires exponential evaluation and normalization, which are costly in hardware. Existing implementations often rely on CORDIC pipelines or BRAM-based look-up tables, increasing area and power consumption. This paper presents a BRAM-free approximate attention-weighting unit for FPGA-based ViT inference. The proposed design approximates the natural exponential in softmax using a 16-segment piecewise-linear function implemented entirely with distributed LUT fabric. Unlike base-2 approximations, the natural-exponential formulation preserves the pre-trained attention temperature and avoids model-specific recalibration. Implemented on a Xilinx Zynq-7020, the complete attention-row core uses 1444 LUTs, 77 DSPs, and no BRAM, while hardware-accurate emulation shows accuracy within a \(0.20\%\) absolute top-1 difference from the exact-softmax reference on ViT-family models. These results demonstrate the potential of the proposed core for energy-efficient ViT inference on resource-constrained edge-AI platforms.

</details>


### [163] [Probabilistic Memory for Trustworthy Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02465)
*Likai Pei,Jiahao Zheng,Xueji Zhao,Emilie Ye,Jianbo Liu,Hanqing Tao,Ming-Yen Lee,Ruiyang Qin,Yiyu Shi,Shimeng Yu,X. Sharon Hu,Ningyuan Cao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出概率存储器(p-MEM)，一种统一存储原语，可直接存储分布参数并以原生内存带宽进行高斯随机数生成(GRNG)，相比传统方案实现超1000 GSa/s/mm²吞吐量，在贝叶斯神经网络任务中减少指令数高达2.19x/4.37x、降低采样延迟562x/3.45x、能耗295.5x/3.53x，为可信概率AI提供可扩展硬件基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率计算在可信边缘智能中至关重要，用于量化不确定性、增强鲁棒性、数据重构和隐私保护，但高斯随机数生成(GRNG)与计算之间存在数量级的数据吞吐量差距，同时指令开销过大，限制了其广泛采用。

Method: 提出概率存储器(p-MEM)原语，该原语统一存储分布参数（如均值和标准差），并以原生内存带宽直接采样，确定性数据作为零方差的特例。通过布局验证的p-MEM模拟器，全面探索器件选择、存储器规格和技术节点。

Result: p-MEM可实现超过1000 GSa/s/mm²的GRNG吞吐量（含存储阵列访问）。集成到CPU/GPU系统后，在贝叶斯神经网络工作负载中，指令数减少高达2.19倍/4.37倍，采样延迟降低562倍/3.45倍，能耗降低295.5倍/3.53倍。

Conclusion: p-MEM为可信概率AI提供了可扩展的硬件基板，通过将随机数生成与存储深度融合，有效弥合了概率计算与数据吞吐之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Probabilistic computation plays an important role in trustworthy edge intelligence to quantify uncertainty, enhance robustness, reconstruct data, and protect privacy, but its adoption is limited by the orders-of-magnitude data throughput gap between Gaussian random number generation (GRNG) and computation, as well as instruction overhead. This paper introduces probabilistic memory (p-MEM), a unified memory primitive that stores distribution parameters, such as mean and standard deviation, and samples directly at the native memory bandwidth, where deterministic data becomes the zero-variance special case. Using a layout-validated p-MEM simulator, we comprehensively explore device choices, memory specifications, and technology nodes, showing that p-MEM can achieve more than 1000 GSa/s/mm^2 GRNG throughput, including memory-array access. Integrated into CPU/GPU systems, p-MEM reduces instruction count by up to 2.19x/4.37x, sampling latency by 562x/3.45x, and energy by 295.5x/3.53x for Bayesian neural network workloads, providing a scalable hardware substrate for trustworthy probabilistic AI.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [PACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Plausible and Actionable Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01306)
*Pavel Iakovets,Liyanapathiranage Sudeepika Wajirakumari Samarathunga,Martin Thomas Horsch,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE，一个模块化神经符号框架，用于生成符合领域知识的可行反事实解释。该框架将神经网络预测与符号推理（ASP规则）分离，在Adult Income数据集上的案例研究表明，符号约束能生成更符合领域可行性要求的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法虽能生成改变预测结果的输入变化，但由于缺乏显式机制来整合领域知识和干预约束，往往产生不切实际或不可行的建议。

Method: 提出PACE模块化神经符号框架，将预测与推理分离为两个组件：神经网络分类模型（预测）和符号推理层（利用ASP规则编码领域约束，在生成反事实时强制执行可行干预）。该框架是模型无关的，可灵活适应不同领域。

Result: 在Adult Income数据集上的案例研究显示：1）符号约束显著提升了反事实解释的领域可行性；2）存在反事实有效性与合理性之间的权衡；3）相比无约束方法，PACE生成的解释更符合领域特定的可行性要求。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法在生成透明、可行感知的反事实解释方面具有潜力，为可解释AI中需要现实决策支持的领域提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations explain machine learning predictions by identifying minimal input changes that would alter a model's decision. Although many existing methods successfully generate prediction-changing alternatives, they often produce unrealistic or infeasible recommendations due to a lack of explicit mechanisms for incorporating domain knowledge and intervention constraints. Neuro-symbolic AI offers a promising direction by combining data-driven predictive models with symbolic reasoning capable of representing human-understandable rules and feasible actions. This paper presents PACE, a modular neuro-symbolic framework for generating feasibility-aware counterfactual explanations. The framework separates prediction and reasoning into two components: a neural predictive model for classification and a symbolic reasoning layer that enforces domain-specific constraints during counterfactual generation. By explicitly modeling feasible interventions, the framework produces explanations consistent with domain knowledge while remaining interpretable and actionable. The approach is model-agnostic and adaptable to domains requiring realistic decision support. A case study is conducted on the Adult Income dataset, combining a multilayer perceptron classifier with Answer Set Programming (ASP) rules encoding feasible modifications to education, occupation, and working hours while preserving immutable attributes. Results highlight the trade-off between counterfactual validity and plausibility and show that symbolic constraints yield explanations that better satisfy domain-specific feasibility requirements, illustrating the potential of neuro-symbolic methods for transparent, feasibility-aware counterfactual explanation in explainable AI.

</details>


### [165] [Auto-FL-Research: Agentic Search for Federated Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01366)
*Holger R. Roth,Ziyue Xu,Chester Chen,Daguang Xu,Peter Cnudde,Andrew Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto-FL-Research (AFR)，一个约束编码Agent工作流，用于自动搜索联邦学习算法配方。在多个医疗和LEAF数据集上评估，结果显示该方法能发现有效的FL算法改进，但也揭示了种子敏感性、固定表面调优效应和单次运行伪影等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习研究中有大量算法选择（优化器、聚合规则、训练调度等），这些选择手动探索成本高且难以公平比较，因此需要自动化方法。

Method: 提出AFR框架，采用约束编码Agent工作流，Agent可提议并实现候选训练算法（包括聚合规则、客户端更新调度、局部目标函数、模型变体），同时任务配置文件固定变异表面、计算预算、通信合约和最终评估。

Result: 在5个FLamby医疗任务和6个LEAF任务上评估，5次重复实验支持4个FLamby任务和5个LEAF任务的性能提升。同时发现部分提升对应FL配方变化，其他提升可通过固定表面标量控制恢复或在重复/保留评估中失败。

Conclusion: Agent生成的候选算法可区分为三类：可重复的FL机制、固定表面调优效应和选出的单次运行伪影。混合结果表明自动化搜索的有效性及其局限性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) research often depends on many small but consequential algorithmic choices: optimizer variants, server aggregation rules, local training schedules, normalization, regularization, and model architecture. These choices are expensive to explore manually and difficult to compare fairly when candidate changes can also alter the FL training or evaluation path. In this work, we present Auto-FL-Research (AFR), a constrained coding-agent workflow for FL algorithmic recipe search. Agents may propose and implement candidate training algorithms, including server aggregation rules, client update schedules, local objectives, and registered model variants, while task profiles fix the mutation surface, compute budget, communication contract, and final model evaluation. Each campaign records candidate scores, runtime, edited files, artifacts, and failure status. We evaluate AFR on five healthcare cross-silo FLamby tasks and on grouped-client profiles for the five fixed LEAF datasets plus the LEAF synthetic task. Five-seed repeat evaluations support gains on four FLamby tasks and five of six LEAF profiles, while also exposing seed-sensitive and search-selected failure cases. Same-budget controls show that several gains correspond to FL-recipe changes, whereas other improvements are recovered by fixed-surface scalar controls or fail under repeat or held-out evaluation. These mixed outcomes are part of the contribution: they show how agent-generated candidates can be separated into repeated FL mechanisms, fixed-surface tuning effects, and selected single-run artifacts.

</details>


### [166] [The Wiola Architecture for Efficient Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01394)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury,Afreen Shaik,Yaparla Bhargavi,Brahma Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wiola 是一个完全原创的小型语言模型架构，包含五种新颖组件：螺旋旋转位置编码（SRPE）、门控跨层注意力（GCLA）、自适应令牌合并（ATM）、双流前馈网络（DSFF）和 WiolaRMSNorm，提供四种规模（120M-1.5B）并兼容 HuggingFace。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型架构（如 GPT、LLaMA、Mistral）结构趋同，缺乏从第一性原理出发的原创设计。作者旨在探索全新的 SLM 架构范式，提出独立于现有模型家族的替代方案。

Method: 提出五种新颖组件：1) SRPE：在三维螺旋流形上嵌入位置信息，融合绝对、相对和层次位置信号；2) GCLA：每层解码器可通过软交叉注意力访问前两层的压缩摘要；3) ATM：在中间层动态合并语义冗余的相邻令牌以降低注意力复杂度；4) DSFF：双并行流替代传统 MLP，由可学习的逐维度门控融合；5) WiolaRMSNorm：引入逐维度可学习偏移向量以防止表示坍塌。

Result: 提供了完整的数学推导、架构框图、复杂度分析，并与 GPT-2、LLaMA-2、Mistral 进行了系统性比较。发布了四种参数规模（120M、360M、700M、1.5B）的模型，全部 22 个架构单元测试通过，兼容 HuggingFace Transformers 生态系统。

Conclusion: Wiola 证明了从第一性原理设计全新 SLM 架构的可行性，其五种独立新颖组件在理论上提供了优于现有架构的潜力，且工程实现完整可靠。

Abstract: We present Wiola, a fully original Small Language Model (SLM) architecture built from first principles, sharing no structural lineage with any existing model family including GPT, LLaMA, Mistral, or Falcon. Wiola introduces five independently novel components: (i) Spiral Rotary Positional Encoding (SRPE), which embeds token positions on a three-dimensional helical manifold combining absolute, relative, and hierarchical positional signals; (ii) Gated Cross-Layer Attention (GCLA), providing each decoder layer with soft cross-attention access to compressed summaries of two preceding layers for inter-layer coherence; (iii) Adaptive Token Merging (ATM), which dynamically merges se mantically redundant adjacent tokens in middle network layers to reduce attention complexity without information loss; (iv) Dual Stream Feed-Forward (DSFF), replacing the conventional MLP with two parallel streams fused by a learned per-dimension gate; and (v) WiolaRMSNorm, a modified normalisation introducing a per-dimension learned offset vector that prevents representation collapse. We provide complete mathematical derivations, architectural block diagrams, complexity analyses, and systematic comparisons against GPT-2, LLaMA-2, and Mistral. Wiola is released in four sizes (120M, 360M, 700M, and 1.5B parameters) and is fully compatible with the HuggingFace Transformers ecosystem, with all 22 architectural unit tests passing.

</details>


### [167] [Agent4cs: A Multi-agent System for Code Summarization in Large Hierarchical Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01425)
*Yongjian Tang,Ezgi Sarikayak,Doruk Tuncel,Jie M. Zhang,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agent4cs是一个多智能体框架，采用自底向上方式对大型代码库进行摘要生成，包含摘要生成、关键词提取和质量保证三个智能体，在7个前沿模型上平均提升语义一致性8%，关键词覆盖率最高提升38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码摘要方案通常依赖单一语言模型或编码助手（如Claude Code），将源码视为纯文本处理，未能充分利用代码库中丰富的依赖关系和层次结构信息，难以应对结构混淆、文档不完整的大型复杂代码库。

Method: 提出多智能体框架Agent4cs，采用自底向上的摘要生成策略：1) 摘要生成智能体（Summarization Agent）负责生成鲁棒摘要；2) 关键词提取智能体（Keyword-Extraction Agent）主动识别子文件夹中的关键信息；3) 质量保证智能体（Quality-Assurance Agent）迭代优化输出的可读性、连贯性和完整性。

Result: 在7个前沿模型上，与两种结构化提示基线方法相比，Agent4cs在所有文件夹层级上平均提升语义一致性8%；在真实世界数据集上，归一化关键词覆盖率最高提升38%。

Conclusion: 多智能体协同的代码摘要方法能有效利用代码库的层次结构和依赖关系，显著优于传统单模型或结构化提示方法，为大型复杂代码库的理解提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Understanding large, complex codebases, especially those with obfuscated structures and incomplete documentation, remains a significant challenge. Existing code summarization solutions often rely on a single language model or coding assistant like Claude Code, and treat source code as flat text, underutilizing the rich interdependencies and hierarchical information within a repository. To address these shortcomings, we propose Agent4cs - a multi-agent framework that summarizes large codebases in a bottom-up fashion, where a summarization agent focuses on producing robust summaries; a keyword-extraction agent proactively identifies critical information from subfolders; and a quality-assurance agent iteratively refines the outputs for readability, coherence, and completeness. Evaluated on 7 frontier models, Agent4cs improves semantic consistency across all folder levels by average 8% compared to two structured prompting baselines with code segments. Furthermore, extensive evaluation on real-world datasets demonstrates up to 38% gains in normalized keyword coverage rate over the same baselines.

</details>


### [168] [When Should Service Agents Reconsider? Difficulty-Routed Control in Customer-Service Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01426)
*Qian Chen,Chengyuan Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种难度路由的服务控制架构，通过轻量级路由器将常规会话保持在低成本基线路径，将操作耦合的会话路由到升级工作流，升级路径使用冲突感知通信和写入触发重新考虑机制，在关键后端写入前集中推理和安全措施，而非对所有服务会话统一应用额外控制。在零售和航空任务上评估，该方法在存在操作冲突的服务请求上持续提高了可靠性，且路由证据表明更强的控制被定向到冲突请求而非常规请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主客服智能体正从对话界面转向操作执行角色（检索企业记录、应用服务策略、执行退款/取消/改签等后端写入），这产生了服务控制问题：企业需要保持常规服务快速低摩擦，同时防止因客户指令、政策约束、企业记录和后端写入交互而导致的操作错误。现有方法缺乏针对操作耦合场景的差异化控制机制。

Method: 提出难度路由服务控制架构：(1) 轻量级路由器将常规会话保持在低成本基线路径，将操作耦合会话路由至升级工作流；(2) 升级路径采用冲突感知通信和写入触发重新考虑机制，在执行关键后端写入前集中推理和安全保障，而非对所有服务会话统一施加额外控制。

Result: 在人类验证的零售和航空任务（τ²-bench）上，零售场景中该方法持续提高了存在操作冲突的服务请求的可靠性。路由证据显示更强控制被定向到冲突请求而非常规请求。对话和工具使用分析表明增益并非来自无差别扩展交互或工具链，而是通过增加对话轮次和工具调用支持证据收集、写入分离和写入前重新考虑。案例分析显示升级工作流保留了备用计划、将检索记录绑定到正确操作、序列化写入、分解多实体请求。航空结果扩展了相同逻辑至预约操作。

Conclusion: 难度路由的服务控制架构能有效区分常规和操作耦合的服务请求，针对性地在关键后端写入前增强推理和安全措施，在保持常规服务效率的同时显著提升操作冲突场景的可靠性，且该方法具有跨领域（零售和航空）的泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous customer-service agents are shifting from conversational interfaces toward operational execution roles: they retrieve firm records, apply service policies, and execute backend writes such as refunds, cancellations, exchanges, order modifications, and reservation changes. This shift creates a service-control problem: firms must keep routine service fast and low-friction while preventing operational errors on requests where customer instructions, policy constraints, firm records, and backend writes interact. We propose a difficulty-routed service-control architecture that asks when service agents should reconsider before acting. A lightweight router keeps routine sessions on a low-cost baseline path and routes operationally coupled sessions to an escalated workflow. The escalated path uses conflict-aware communication and write-triggered reconsideration to concentrate deliberation and safeguards before consequential backend writes, rather than applying additional control uniformly across all service sessions. We evaluate the architecture on human-verified retail and airline tasks from $τ^{2}$-bench. In retail, the method improves reliability consistently on service requests with operational conflict. Routing evidence shows that stronger control is directed toward conflicted requests rather than broadly applied to routine ones. Dialogue and tool-use profiles suggest that gains do not come from indiscriminate interaction expansion or broader tool chains; instead, added turns and tool calls support evidence gathering, write separation, and pre-write reconsideration. Case-level evidence shows that the escalated workflow preserves fallback plans, binds retrieved records to the correct action, sequences writes, and decomposes multi-entity requests. Airline results extend the same service-control logic to reservation operations.

</details>


### [169] [CreativityNeuro: Steering Language Model Weights to Improve Divergent Thinking and Reduce Mode Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01433)
*Samuel Schapiro,Core Francisco Park,Felix Sosa,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CreativityNeuro是一种无需数据的方法，通过对比权重引导增强LLM的 divergent thinking，在多项创造力测试中显著提升性能并减少模式崩溃，且能泛化到未见任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在开放性问题中倾向于生成相似回答（人工蜂群效应），缺乏 divergent thinking（发散性思维），需要提升其创造力。

Method: 提出 CreativityNeuro，一种无需数据的方法，通过对比权重引导（contrastive weight steering）在权重空间而非激活空间进行干预，增强LLM的发散性思维。

Result: 在DAT任务上提升高达14个人类百分位点；在AUT和Task Task的大规模人类评估（N=720）中，原创性、惊喜度和创造力显著提升；在所有三项任务上可度量地减少模式崩溃。激活引导在DAT上表现相当但无法泛化到AUT和Task Task。

Conclusion: CreativityNeuro无需行为数据、重新训练或梯度微调即可有效提升LLM在创造性领域的发散性思维并减少模式崩溃，提供了一种简洁有效的增强方案。

Abstract: Divergent thinking is a crucial aspect of creativity, yet large language models (LLMs) tend to consistently generate similar responses to open-ended questions, in what has been termed the artificial hivemind effect. Here, we introduce CreativityNeuro, a data-free method for enhancing divergent thinking in LLMs via contrastive weight steering. We evaluate our method across multiple creativity assessments and report several main findings. On the Divergent Association Task (DAT), a vocabulary-space creativity test, CreativityNeuro improves performance by up to 14 human percentile points. Next, in a large-scale human evaluation (N=720) on the Alternative Uses Test (AUT) and the Task Task, CreativityNeuro achieves significant improvements in originality, surprise, and creativity, transferring to longer-form and more open-ended tasks. Importantly, we find that across all three tasks, CreativityNeuro demonstrably reduces measures of mode collapse. Moreover, activation steering achieves comparable performance to CreativityNeuro on the DAT, but it does not transfer to the AUT and Task Task, demonstrating the effectiveness of weight-space steering in generalizing to unseen tasks. In conclusion, CreativityNeuro improves divergent thinking and reduces mode collapse without requiring behavioral data, re-training, or gradient-based fine-tuning, providing a straightforward way to enhance LLM performance in creative domains.

</details>


### [170] [Discrete Diffusion Language Models for Interactive Radiology Report Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01436)
*Max Van Puyvelde,Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Wim Van Criekinge,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将扩散语言模型（DiffusionGemma-26B）应用于医学视觉问答，在LoRA微调下与同规模自回归模型（Gemma-4-26B）性能相当或更优，解码速度快3.5-4.4倍，并具备自回归模型缺乏的任意顺序填充能力，更适合医学报告生成场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学基础模型几乎全部采用自回归生成范式，而扩散语言模型在通用文本生成中已展现出与自回归模型相当甚至更优的性能，但在医学领域尚未得到充分探索。作者旨在验证扩散模型在医学视觉问答中的有效性及其独特优势。

Method: 采用混合专家扩散语言模型DiffusionGemma-26B，在医学视觉问答数据集上使用相同LoRA配方进行微调，与同尺寸自回归模型Gemma-4-26B进行对照实验，使用对冗余度鲁棒的LLM裁判进行评分。

Result: 扩散模型在所有医学视觉问答数据集上表现与自回归模型相当或更优；微调模型（3.8B活跃参数）与前沿视觉语言模型具有竞争力；解码速度快3.5-4.4倍；具备任意顺序填充能力，在医学报告片段填充任务中表现优于自回归模型。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型在医学视觉问答中不仅达到与自回归模型同等的性能水平，还提供了显著的速度优势和独特的任意顺序填充能力，这种双向去噪特性天然适合医学报告生成中常见的片段不完整或不一致的场景。

Abstract: Diffusion language models, which generate text by denoising a token canvas bidirectionally instead of emitting tokens left to right, have become competitive with autoregressive (AR) generation. Medical foundation models, however, remain almost entirely autoregressive. We adapt a mixture-of-experts diffusion language model, DiffusionGemma-26B, and benchmark it against its same-size AR sibling Gemma-4-26B under an identical LoRA recipe on medical visual question answering datasets, scored by a verbosity-robust LLM judge. Diffusion matches or exceeds AR on all of them, and the finetuned model (3.8B active) is competitive with frontier vision-language models; its decoding is also 3.5-4.4x faster. Beyond this parity, the diffusion model offers a drafting capability AR lacks: any-order infill. Because the canvas is denoised bidirectionally, a radiologist can fix report fragments and have the model fill the text between them, an operation inherent to diffusion but not to autoregression, which is subpar at it. This suits real reports, which are often terse or inconsistent across clinicians and institutions.

</details>


### [171] [Beyond Next-Token Prediction: An RLVR Proof of Concept for Tool-Use Agents on Atlassian Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01465)
*Karthikeya Aditya Vissa,Sankalp Mane,Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大型语言模型在特定企业SaaS API工作流中因训练目标（预测下一个token）与实际任务（正确调用API端点）不匹配导致的静默失败问题，提出使用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在目标环境中直接训练。在五个高保真合成Jira/Confluence环境上的实验表明，RLVR训练的小模型（Qwen3.5-4B）在四个非退化场景中将平均奖励从0.35-0.92提升至0.95-1.00，最大提升来自Confluence页面创建（0.35→1.00）。但该方法存在手动构造奖励函数不可扩展和一个场景奖励饱和两个主要局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的标准自回归训练目标（预测下一个token）与在企业SaaS API工作流中需要正确调用端点、嵌套参数和保持调用顺序的实际需求之间存在目标错配。这种错配导致模型在调用API时出现静默失败（遗漏必填字段、幻觉工具、过早停止），而传统的监督微调或提示工程难以彻底解决这类序列决策问题。

Method: 构建五个高保真合成环境（模拟Jira REST v3和Confluence v2的API模式），设计基于工具调用轨迹的可验证奖励函数（无需真实API、学习型裁判或人工标注）。使用GRPO算法对Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3.5-4B进行强化学习训练，奖励信号由与训练时相同的检查器计算。

Result: 在四个非退化奖励场景中，RLVR训练的策略将平均奖励从4B基线的0.35-0.92提升至0.95-1.00。Confluence页面创建场景提升最大（0.35→1.00）。但"票据状态转换"场景的奖励函数已饱和，提示基线已取得满分。

Conclusion: RLVR方法在小模型上能有效弥合目标错配，在特定企业API调用任务上取得接近完美的表现。但手写可验证奖励函数难以扩展到少量端点之外的场景，且部分任务存在奖励饱和问题。本文定位为面向小众企业API的成果优化小模型的初步探索。

Abstract: Large language models are trained to predict the next token, not to act inside a specific API. In niche enterprise SaaS workflows -- where success means hitting the right endpoint with the right nested arguments in the right order -- this objective mismatch shows up as silent failures: dropped required fields, hallucinated tools, or early stops after a single read. We ask whether Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), applied directly in the target environment, closes the gap. As a proof of concept we build a suite of five synthetic environments emulating the Jira REST v3 and Confluence v2 APIs at schema fidelity; rewards are computed entirely from the tool-call trace, with no live API, no learned judge, and no human label in the loop. Scoring prompted Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3.5-4B on the same checkers that drive GRPO training, we find that on the four scenarios whose rewards are non-degenerate the RL-trained policy lifts average reward from a 4B-baseline range of 0.35--0.92 to 0.95--1.00, with the largest single gain on Confluence page creation ($0.35 \rightarrow 1.00$). We position this as a preliminary step toward outcome-optimised small models for niche enterprise APIs, and foreground two limitations a workshop reader should weigh: hand-crafting verifiable rewards does not scale beyond the handful of endpoints reported here, and one of our five scenarios (ticket-transition) has a saturating reward shape that the prompted 4B already maxes out.

</details>


### [172] [World Feedback for Clinical Agents: Diagnosing RL in FHIR Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01470)
*Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Prasanna Desikan,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过构建MedAgentBench-v3基准（508个任务，8.9%静默完成上限），审计发现MedAgentBench v1/v2存在41.7%的静默完成上限问题导致强化学习（RL）中不作为成为主导策略。训练Qwen3-8B揭示两个结构性障碍：能力上限（10/20任务类型零基础性能）和格式知识障碍（3/20类型需要精确临床代码无法通过探索发现）。纯RL达到18.2% pass@1，而基于规则的SFT为34.1%，15.9个百分点的差距完全归因于这些障碍。提出的决策/格式知识/查找分类法可预测RL可学习性，并给出解决方案：SFT注入代码知识，RL学习条件逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床协议执行任务（如检查化验值、应用阈值、生成FHIR订单）天然适合从世界反馈中进行强化学习（RL），因为临床专家可将决策逻辑编码为验证器，验证器可对无限次 rollout 进行评分而无需每个情节的人工标注。但应用RL需要合理的反馈通道和足够的基础能力。本文旨在审计现有基准的问题并构建更合适的基准，同时识别RL应用于此类任务的结构性障碍。

Method: 1. 审计MedAgentBench v1/v2，发现41.7%的静默完成上限问题。2. 构建MedAgentBench-v3（MAB-v3），包含508个任务，静默完成上限降至8.9%。3. 训练Qwen3-8B模型进行实验分析。4. 提出决策/格式知识/查找分类法用于预测RL可学习性。

Result: 纯RL达到18.2% pass@1，基于规则的SFT达到34.1%，15.9个百分点差距完全归因于能力上限和格式知识障碍。10/20任务类型零基础性能，3/20类型需要精确临床代码无法通过探索发现。

Conclusion: RL应用于临床协议执行任务存在两个结构性障碍（能力上限和格式知识障碍），需要结合SFT注入代码知识和RL学习条件逻辑的组合策略来解决。

Abstract: Clinical protocol-execution tasks -- checking a lab value, applying a threshold, placing a correctly structured FHIR order -- are natural candidates for RL from world feedback: once clinical SMEs encode decision logic into a verifier, that verifier grades unlimited rollouts without per-episode annotation. But applying RL requires a sound feedback channel and sufficient base capability. We audit MedAgentBench v1/v2, find a 41.7\% silent-finish ceiling that makes inaction the RL dominant strategy, and construct \textbf{MedAgentBench-v3 (MAB-v3)} (508 tasks, 8.9\% ceiling). Training Qwen3-8B exposes two structural barriers: a \emph{capability ceiling} (10/20 task types have 0\% base performance, zero gradient) and a \emph{format-knowledge barrier} (3/20 types require exact clinical codes undiscoverable by exploration). Pure RL reaches 18.2\% pass@1 vs.\ 34.1\% for rule-based SFT; the 15.9~pp gap is attributable entirely to these barriers. A decision/format-knowledge/lookup taxonomy predicts RL learnability and prescribes the fix: SFT to inject codes, RL to learn conditionals.

</details>


### [173] [Procedural Memory Distillation: Online Reflection for Self-Improving Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01480)
*Ye Liu,Srijan Bansal,Bo Pang,Yang Li,Zeyu Leo Liu,Yifei Ming,Zixuan Ke,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出程序记忆蒸馏（PMD），通过跨回合信号构建三层抽象的程序记忆（原始轨迹、自我反思策略、跨问题行为模式），并利用记忆条件化的自教师机制将程序知识蒸馏到策略参数中，实现无记忆推理。在Qwen3-8B和OLMo3-Instruct-7B上，PMD相较于SDPO在SCIKNOWEVAL提升3.8-5.5%，在LIVECODEBENCH提升7.9-13.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR及其自蒸馏变体（如SDPO）仅利用回合级的验证信号更新策略，忽略了回合中丰富的程序性信息（如哪些策略持续通过验证、哪些失败模式反复出现），且跨回合信号无法被局部更新捕获。

Method: PMD构建三层抽象的程序记忆：原始轨迹、自我反思策略与教训、跨问题的高层行为模式。记忆从模型自身轨迹在线提取，记忆条件化的自教师利用累积经验监督学生自身 rollout，使学生在参数中内化程序知识。核心设计原则是共同进化：策略生成轨迹更新记忆，记忆塑造监督更新策略。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和OLMo3-Instruct-7B上，PMD较SDPO在SCIKNOWEVAL提升3.8-5.5%，在LIVECODEBENCH提升7.9-13.6%。消融实验表明，冻结记忆或策略均导致性能下降超过10%。

Conclusion: 跨回合的程序性记忆蒸馏能有效提升策略学习效率，共同进化的记忆-策略双更新机制是性能提升的关键。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), along with recent selfdistillation variants such as SDPO, evaluates each rollout against a verifier and updates the policy from that episode-level signal. However, the richer procedural information in the rollout is rarely retained or reused. Across episodes and epochs, the model repeatedly encounters related problems under a changing policy, producing cross-episode signals that episode-local updates cannot capture: which strategies consistently pass verification, which failure modes persist, which patterns recur. We propose Procedural Memory Distillation (PMD), which converts these crossepisode signals into reusable procedural memory and distills it into the policy's weights during training. This memory functions as a training scaffold, absorbed into the policy itself, yielding a memory-free model at inference. PMD organizes the memory at three levels of abstraction: raw trajectories, self-reflected strategies and lessons, and higher-level behavioral patterns that recur across problems, all extracted online from the model's own trajectories. A memory-conditioned self-teacher draws on the accumulated experience to supervise the student on its own rollouts, enabling student to progressively internalize procedural knowledge within its parameters. The central design principle is co-evolution: the policy generates rollouts that update the memory, and memory shapes the supervision that updates the policy. Empirically, across Qwen3-8B and OLMo3-Instruct-7B, PMD improves over SDPO by 3.8-5.5% on SCIKNOWEVAL and 7.9-13.6% on LIVECODEBENCH. Co-evolution powers these gains: freezing either the memory or the policy trails PMD by more than 10% across SCIKNOWEVAL domains.

</details>


### [174] [The Agentic Garden of Forking Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01507)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jonathan K Pritchard,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI智能体能够复现人类研究者的分析变异性，暴露出方法论分歧导致的结论差异。通过角色分配，AI可生成不同乃至对立的结论。在42个团队分析同一移民数据的研究中，AI复现了72%的人类意识形态差距。多数AI报告能通过同行评审。作者提出m值（多元宇宙值）和智能体自助法（Agentic Bootstrap）来评估分析路径的极端性，主张科学证据应基于合理分析分布而非单一分析来评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证研究中不同的分析选择会导致不同结论，但这类"隐藏的分叉路径"难以被观察。作者希望利用AI智能体显式地捕捉和再现人类研究者之间的分析变异性，从而揭示方法论选择对结论的系统性影响。

Method: 1) 让分配不同角色的AI智能体分析同一数据，观察结论分歧。2) 在42个人类团队分析同一移民数据的研究中，让AI智能体重现分析，比较与人类结论的差异。3) 对AI报告进行独立AI评审和人类专家评审。4) 提出m值（multiverse value）衡量分析路径的极端性。5) 提出Agentic Bootstrap方法，利用AI智能体对合理分析路径进行采样，估计m值。

Result: AI智能体在不同角色设定下得出不同甚至对立的结论。AI复现了72%的人类意识形态差距。86%的AI报告通过独立AI评审，78%通过多数人类专家评审。在人类移民研究中，13.5%的已发表分析属于分析空间中最极端的5%（m<0.05）。

Conclusion: 研究中的核心问题往往不是分析本身有缺陷，而是从大量方法论上合理的分析中选择性地探索和报告。AI可能通过使这种探索变得廉价和可扩展而放大问题。科学证据不应仅基于单一报告分析来评估，而应基于其在合理可报告分析分布中的位置。Agentic Bootstrap使这种分布可观察，并将其作为科学可信度的标准。

Abstract: Empirical research rarely admits a unique analysis. Different analytical choices can lead to different conclusions from the same data, yet these hidden forking paths are difficult to observe. We show that AI agents capture much of the analytical variation among human researchers while making these paths explicit. Across four high-stakes domains, assigning different personas is sufficient for AI agents to report divergent, often opposing, conclusions from the same data and question, with findings systematically aligned with those beliefs. In a study in which 42 human research teams analyzed the same immigration dataset, AI agents reproduced 72% of the human ideological gap in reported effect estimates. Despite reaching opposing conclusions, it is difficult to identify clear issues in each analysis based on the final AI reports: 86% passed independent AI review and 78% passed majority human expert review. These findings suggest that the central challenge is often not flawed analyses, but selective exploration and reporting from a large space of methodologically defensible analyses. AI agents may amplify this longstanding problem by making such exploration inexpensive and scalable. To address this, we introduce the m-value (multiverse value), the probability that an analysis path would produce a claim at least as extreme as the reported one. We further introduce Agentic Bootstrap, which estimates the m-value by using AI agents to sample plausible analysis paths. Applied to the human immigration study, 13.5% of reported human analyses fell in the most extreme 5% of the analysis space (m<0.05). Scientific evidence should therefore be evaluated not only by a single reported analysis but also by its position within the distribution of analyses that could reasonably have been reported. Agentic Bootstrap makes this distribution observable and turns it into a criterion for scientific credibility.

</details>


### [175] [Janus: a Playground for User-Involved Agentic Permission Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01510)
*Natalie Grace Brigham,Eugene Bagdasarian,Tadayoshi Kohno,Franziska Roesner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Janus是一个用于实现和评估用户参与的AI代理权限管理设计的游乐场系统，包含Janus-Core模块化代理系统和Janus-Harness自动评估框架。研究发现用户参与对隐私安全至关重要，AI辅助可减轻认知负担，且需考虑权限疲劳等现实用户行为，没有单一设计在所有场景中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有多种权限管理方法，但用户在执行工具调用的AI代理系统中的角色（用户能做什么、应该做什么）尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 构建Janus系统（包括Janus-Core模块化代理系统和Janus-Harness自动评估框架），基于概念模型识别用户参与的关键设计轴，实现六种涵盖设计空间的权限助手，并在三个场景和三个合成响应者上进行评估。

Result: 用户输入对增强隐私和安全至关重要；AI增强用户决策可减轻认知负荷；现实用户行为（如权限疲劳）必须在系统设计中考虑；没有单一设计在所有上下文中表现最优。

Conclusion: 需要采用更原则化、上下文感知的方法来在代理系统中部署权限助手，Janus已开源以支持该方向的未来研究。

Abstract: AI agents that autonomously execute tool calls on a user's behalf raise pressing questions about permission management: what role could users play, and what role should they play? Despite many proposed approaches, the user's role in agentic permission management remains under explored. We introduce Janus, a playground system for implementing and evaluating user-involved agentic permission management designs. Janus consists of two components: Janus-Core, a modular agentic system supporting a diverse spectrum of permission management designs, and Janus-Harness, an automated evaluation framework. Grounded in a conceptual model that identifies key design axes for user involvement, we implement six permission assistants spanning the design space and evaluate them across three scenarios and three synthetic responders. We demonstrate that user input is critical and can significantly strengthen privacy and security, that AI augmentation of user decisions can help reduce cognitive load, and that realistic user behavior including permission fatigue must be accounted for in system design. No single design performs optimally across all contexts, motivating a more principled and context-sensitive approach to deploying permission assistants in agentic systems. Janus is publicly available to support future investigation into this dimension of agentic system design.

</details>


### [176] [Revisiting Chain-of-Thought Reasoning under Limited Supervision: Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01511)
*Hongyang He,Jiuming Liu,Victor Sanchez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文定义了半监督思维链学习（Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning），并提出了Semi-CoT框架，通过无标注问题生成伪推理监督信号，利用答案级语义熵筛选低熵推理链作为可靠伪思维链示例。实验表明框架能选出高精度伪思维链，但在下游任务上效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有思维链推理方法大多仅将推理链作为推理时的提示使用，很少将生成的推理踪迹作为半监督学习信号加以复用，因此本文探索如何利用无标注问题来构建伪推理监督。

Method: Semi-CoT对每个无标注问题采样多个伪思维链，通过估计答案级语义熵，选择低熵推理链作为可靠的伪思维链示例，从而实现从推理时优化到半监督伪监督的扩展。

Result: 熵门控机制选择的伪思维链的伪答案精度在91.36%到100%之间；Semi-CoT在SVAMP和GSM8K上取得小幅提升，AQuA出现负迁移，MultiArith达到性能上限。

Conclusion: 无标注问题可以提供可靠的伪推理信号，但要有效利用仍需更强的示例选择策略或学生模型训练方法。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has emerged as an effective approach for activating latent reasoning capabilities in large language models. However, most existing CoT methods use reasoning chains mainly as inference-time prompts, while the generated reasoning traces are rarely reused as semi-supervised learning signals. In this report, we define \textbf{Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning} and propose \textbf{Semi-CoT}, a simple framework that uses unlabeled questions to construct pseudo reasoning supervision. Semi-CoT samples multiple pseudo-CoTs for each unlabeled question, estimates answer-level semantic entropy, and selects low-entropy reasoning chains as reliable pseudo-CoT demonstrations. This extends the self-training view of CoT from inference-time refinement to semi-supervised pseudo-supervision. Pilot experiments on AQuA, SVAMP, GSM8K, and MultiArith show that the entropy gate selects high-precision pseudo-CoTs, with pseudo-answer precision ranging from $91.36\%$ to $100\%$. Semi-CoT also gives small gains on SVAMP and GSM8K, while AQuA shows negative transfer and MultiArith reaches a ceiling. These results suggest that unlabeled questions can provide reliable pseudo reasoning signals, but their effective use still requires stronger demonstration selection or student training.

</details>


### [177] [OPINE-World: Programmatic World Modeling with Ontology-error-Prioritized Interactive Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01531)
*David Courtis,Wenhao Li,Scott Sanner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OPINE-World，一种结合LLM程序合成与对象中心化世界模型在线学习的智能体，通过假设-测试循环、重放验证和基于贝叶斯本体论误差的探索机制，在ARC-AGI-3基准上无需逐游戏训练即解决20/25个游戏，动作效率达78.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络世界模型数据效率低且分布外泛化差，而LLM程序合成世界模型虽数据高效但此前仅适用于结构化状态环境（需预定义对象词汇），无法扩展到需灵活假设对象结构的像素渲染环境。

Method: 提出OPINE-World框架，包含两个协作代理：一个在环境中执行动作，另一个合成程序化世界模型。采用CEGIS循环，结合重放验证和基于模型的规划，并以贝叶斯本体论误差（ontology error）度量引导探索，实现对象类别自适应。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-3基准上，OPINE-World无需逐游戏训练即解决20/25个游戏，动作效率得分78.4（人类基线对比）。

Conclusion: 将程序合成世界模型扩展到对象结构需灵活推断的像素级环境是可行的，贝叶斯本体论误差有效指导了探索，展示出面向新任务的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Learning how an environment behaves from interaction is central to building agents that adapt to unfamiliar tasks. World models learned with deep networks are flexible but data-hungry and transfer poorly beyond their training distribution. Program-synthesized world models, written as source code by LLMs and refined through counterexample-guided inductive synthesis (CEGIS), are instead data-efficient and reusable, yet they have been demonstrated mainly on structured-state worlds with a given object vocabulary, and a single program search does not scale to pixel-rendered environments whose object structure must be hypothesized flexibly. We introduce OPINE-World, an LLM agent that learns an object-centric programmatic world model online from interaction. OPINE-World couples two cooperating agents in a loop of hypothesis and test, one acting in the environment and one synthesizing the model in code with replay verification and model-based planning, and it steers exploration with a Bayesian measure of object-type adequacy we call ontology error. We evaluate OPINE-World on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark for skill-acquisition efficiency in which the object vocabulary, the goal, and the action semantics are withheld. OPINE-World solves 20 of 25 games without per-game training and reaches an action-efficiency score of 78.4 against the human baseline.

</details>


### [178] [Scaling Trends for Lie Detector Oversight in Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01567)
*Oskar J. Hollinsworth,Ann-Kathrin Dombrowski,Sam Adam-Day,Adam Gleave,Chris Cundy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了SOLiD（通过谎言检测器实现可扩展监督）方法，在更大模型（1B到405B参数）和更多样的偏好学习场景中评估。结果显示有利的扩展性：在99%检测器真阳性率下，未检测到的欺骗从34%（1B）降至14%（405B）。但SOLiD对训练数据分布偏移敏感，会导致误报率上升到不可行水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中的欺骗行为监控成本高昂，需要更高效的监督方法。SOLiD通过谎言检测器识别需审查的响应，但先前评估仅限于小模型和有限场景。

Method: 将SOLiD扩展到更大规模模型（1B-405B参数），在更多样化和真实的偏好学习环境中进行评估。分析检测器训练数据与偏好训练数据之间的分布偏移对性能的影响。

Result: 未检测到的欺骗率从1B模型的34%降至405B模型的14%（99%真阳性率下）；可完全移除昂贵的人工标注员而不产生统计显著的欺骗增加；但分布偏移会导致检测器假阳性率上升到不可行水平。

Conclusion: SOLiD在更大模型上表现出有利的扩展性，且有可能在微调阶段完全省去人工标注员。但其对分布偏移的敏感性是实际部署中需要解决的关键挑战。

Abstract: Deceptive behavior in LLMs is costly to monitor and prevent, motivating approaches such as Scalable Oversight via Lie Detectors (SOLiD) (Cundy & Gleave, 2025), which uses lie detectors to identify responses for review by high-cost labelers. In this paper, we scale SOLiD to larger models and evaluate it in more diverse and realistic preference-learning settings. We find favorable scaling: undetected deception drops from 34% for 1B-parameter models to 14% for 405B-parameter models at a detector true positive rate of 99%, and expensive human labelers can be removed entirely from the fine-tuning phase without a statistically significant increase in deception. However, SOLiD is sensitive to distribution shift between detector training and preference-training data, which can drive detector false positive rates to impractical levels.

</details>


### [179] [EO-Agents: A Three-Agent LLM Pipeline for Earth Observation Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01584)
*Mahyar Ghazanfari,Amin Tabrizian,Armin Mehrabian,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于NASA地球观测知识图谱的假设生成管道，利用异构图表征学习与多智能体LLM协作，从1,475个数据集中生成了160个跨学科的科学假设，并通过实验揭示了LLM评估中法官身份偏差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型科学假设生成方法多依赖非结构化文献和自由文本声明，缺乏对结构化知识图谱的利用，难以保证假设的可验证性和领域的系统性。

Method: 采用异构图表征神经网络在历史共现关系上训练以排序候选数据集配对，随后通过三智能体LLM流水线（过滤、生成、评估）构建结构化研究假设。

Result: 系统从1,475个NASA数据集中产出160个跨领域假设，模型预测的新数据集配对与真实文献中的共现案例在合理性上接近；2×2×2析因实验表明假设排名稳定但绝对评分受评审者身份影响显著。

Conclusion: 该管道能够生成科学连贯且尚未被探索的数据组合假设，但LLM评估存在法官身份偏差问题，单一评审者的评估方式具有局限性。

Abstract: Large language models have recently been explored for scientific hypothesis generation, but most prior work relies on unstructured literature and free-form textual claims. We present a pipeline for Earth observation that grounds hypothesis generation directly in the NASA Earth Observation Knowledge Graph. A heterogeneous graph neural network trained on historical co-usage relations ranks candidate dataset pairings, and a three-agent LLM pipeline filters, generates, and evaluates structured research hypotheses. Applied to 1,475 NASA datasets, the system produces 160 hypotheses spanning multiple Earth-science domains, including ecohydrology, glaciology, aerosol--cloud interactions, vegetation phenology, and stratospheric chemistry. Model-predicted novel dataset pairings are rated nearly as plausible as held-out real co-usages from the literature, indicating that the pipeline surfaces scientifically coherent yet unexplored combinations. A 2*2*2 factorial experiment across GPT-5.2 and Claude Sonnet 4.6 shows that hypothesis rankings remain stable, while absolute scores depend strongly on judge identity, highlighting limitations of single-judge LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [180] [Hawk: Harnessing Hardware-Aware Knowledge for High-Performance NPU Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01590)
*Junyi Wen,Ruiyan Zhuang,Yongjia Xu,Pengtu Li,Rui Zou,Hongyi Chen,Chingman Wan,Puxu Yang,Wuhui Chen,Yanlin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Hawk是一个无需训练的框架，通过三大核心模块（运行时知识合成、瓶颈感知知识检索、效果驱动知识蒸馏）将硬件感知知识注入大语言模型，为NPU生成高性能内核，将生成准确率从49.4%提升至80.0%，执行速度最高提升2.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NPU高性能内核开发是行业瓶颈，开发者需手动处理隐式硬件约束和严格内存层级。大语言模型虽具自动化潜力，但因缺乏硬件特定先验知识，在NPU上生成的内核虽能通过编译却常引发运行时崩溃和性能下降。

Method: Hawk采用三模块框架：(1)运行时知识合成模块，通过三元可执行知识表示将错误上下文与可执行语义耦合；(2)瓶颈感知知识检索模块，采用2D检索范式将查询投影到正交的句法空间和硬件对齐语义空间；(3)效果驱动知识蒸馏模块，利用LLM驱动的语义仲裁，基于经验执行反馈不断剪枝错误、合并冗余以蒸馏知识。

Result: 在真实NPU负载上的评估显示，Hawk将生成准确率从49.4%提升至80.0%，执行速度相较现有最优基线最高提升2.2倍。

Conclusion: 通过硬件感知知识的有效注入，Hawk显著提升了LLM在NPU内核生成任务上的可行性和性能，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Developing high-performance kernels for Neural Processing Units (NPUs) is a critical industry bottleneck, requiring developers to manually navigate implicit hardware constraints and strict memory hierarchies. While large language models offer immense automation potential, they fail catastrophically on NPUs due to a fundamental lack of hardware-specific priors. Naively transplanting code snippets from similar NPU kernels may pass the compiler, but it consistently triggers runtime crashes and performance degradation by blindly violating underlying hardware constraints. To overcome this, we introduce Hawk, a training-free framework that harnesses hardware-aware knowledge through three core modules: (1) Run-Time Knowledge Synthesis Module, which employs a Triple-Part Executable Knowledge Representation to inherently couple the error context with executable semantics; (2) Bottleneck-Aware Knowledge Retrieval Module, which implements a 2D-Retrieval paradigm to project queries into orthogonal syntactic and hardware-aligned semantic spaces; and (3) Effect-Driven Knowledge Distillation Module, which leverages LLM-driven semantic arbitration to continuously distill the knowledge by pruning errors and consolidating redundancies based on the empirical execution feedback. Extensive evaluations on real-world NPU workloads demonstrate that Hawk elevates generation accuracy from 49.4% to 80.0%, while achieving up to a 2.2x execution speedup over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [181] [Safe and Adaptive Cloud Healing: Verifying LLM-Generated Recovery Plans with a Neural-Symbolic World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01595)
*Junyan Tan,Haoran Lin,Siyuan Guo,Yichen Fang,Xinyue Luo,Tianyu Shen,Zeyu Qiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PASE是一个将故障自愈重构为神经符号程序合成任务的新型框架，通过LLM规划引擎、神经符号世界模型和DRL训练的元提示优化器形成紧密的推理-规划-验证-自适应循环，在真实云故障数据集上实现恢复时间降低40%以上，并提升未知故障场景的检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽结合LLM的语义理解能力和DRL的策略优化能力，但采用顺序松耦合架构，未能充分利用LLM的生成与推理能力，导致故障自愈效率受限。

Method: PASE将LLM作为核心规划合成引擎，从语义基元库生成结构化恢复计划；神经符号世界模型通过仿真验证计划可行性；元提示优化器经DRL训练学习生成最优提示以引导LLM的规划过程，形成紧密的推理-规划-验证-自适应闭环。

Result: 在真实云故障注入数据集上，PASE相比现有方法将平均系统恢复时间降低超过40%，并在未知故障场景中提升了故障检测精度。

Conclusion: PASE通过统一LLM推理、模型辅助验证和元学习引导，实现了动态、上下文感知的恢复策略生成，推动了自主系统管理的前沿发展。

Abstract: As the scale and complexity of cloud-based AI systems continue to escalate, ensuring service reliability through rapid fault detection and adaptive recovery has become a critical challenge. While existing approaches integrate Large Language Models (LLMs) for semantic understanding and Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) for policy optimization, they often rely on sequential, loosely coupled architectures that underutilize the generative and reasoning capabilities of LLMs. In this paper, we propose a paradigm shift with PASE, a Planning-Aware Semantic self-healing engine, a novel fault self-healing framework that reconceptualizes recovery as a neuro-symbolic program synthesis task. PASE employs an LLM as a core Plan Synthesis Engine to generate structured recovery plans from a library of semantic primitives. A Neural-Symbolic World Model verifies plan feasibility through simulation, while a Meta-Prompt Optimizer, trained via DRL, learns to generate optimal prompts that guide the LLM's planning process. This tight reason-plan-verify-adapt loop enables dynamic, context-aware recovery strategy generation beyond predefined action spaces. Experiments on a real-world cloud fault injection dataset demonstrate that PASE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, reducing average system recovery time by over 40% and improving fault detection accuracy in unknown fault scenarios. Our framework advances autonomous system management by unifying LLM-based reasoning with model-assisted verification and meta-learned guidance.

</details>


### [182] [SemHash-LLM: A Multi-Granularity Semantic Hashing Framework for Document Deduplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01601)
*Xinyi Fang,Kejian Tong,Jiabei Liu,Tao Ning,Yuhang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemHash LLM 提出了一种多粒度文档去重框架，融合语义投影哈希、注意力加权 MinHash、对比边界学习和选择性 LLM 裁决，在保持语义等价性的同时实现高效的大规模去重，且神经验证成本低于 1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模文档去重需要在保持语义等价性的同时，在海量语料上保持高效，现有方法难以兼顾这些目标。

Method: 提出了 SemHash LLM 多粒度框架：1）语义投影哈希在蒸馏LLM嵌入空间中学习紧凑二进制码；2）注意力加权 MinHash 抑制模板噪音、突出信息性内容；3）对比边界学习和不确定性估计提升鲁棒性；4）选择性 LLM 裁决作为级联过滤管道的最后环节，控制神经验证成本。

Result: 在模板污染、短文本扰动、文本包含和病毒式传播片段等多种场景下取得强去重质量，神经验证成本低于 1%。

Conclusion: 多粒度融合与级联过滤的设计能够有效平衡去重质量与计算效率，是可扩展的大规模语义去重方案。

Abstract: Large scale document deduplication must preserve semantic equivalence while remaining efficient over massive corpora. We present SemHash LLM, a multi granularity framework that unifies semantic projection hashing, attention weighted MinHash, contrastive boundary learning, and selective LLM based adjudication. The method combines character, token, and document level signals through gated fusion, then applies a cascaded filtering pipeline for efficient candidate reduction. Semantic projection hashing learns compact binary codes in distilled LLM embedding space, while attention weighted Min- Hash suppresses boilerplate and emphasizes informative content. Adaptive decision boundaries and uncertainty estimation further improve robustness across template pollution, short text perturbation, containment, and viral fragments. Experiments show that SemHash LLM achieves strong duplicate detection quality with less than one percent neural verification cost.

</details>


### [183] [Profit-Based Counterfactual Explanations for Product Improvement: A Case Study of Manga Sales in Japan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01610)
*Keita Kinjo,Takeshi Ebina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于利润的反事实解释（PBCE）框架，将反事实解释重新定义为利润最大化问题，无需外部指定目标值，并将距离度量重新解释为修改产品属性的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法在回归设置中存在目标值指定有效性和距离度量实际解释性不足的问题，且多数方法只关注改变预测结果而非优化决策目标，难以满足现实管理决策中的目标最大化需求。

Method: 将反事实解释构建为利润最大化问题，以利润最大化替代传统的外生目标指定，并将距离项重新解释为产品属性修改成本，提出PBCE框架。

Result: PBCE无需外生目标指定，直接以利润最大化为优化目标，同时赋予距离度量明确的经济学解释。

Conclusion: PBCE框架在管理和营销场景中提供了更符合实际决策需求的反事实解释方法，解决了现有方法在目标指定和距离解释方面的局限。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanation (CE) is widely used to enhance the interpretability of machine learning models and support data-driven decision-making based on model predictions. However, existing CE methods typically require two exogenously specified inputs: a desired output value (target) and a distance function that quantifies changes in explanatory variables. In regression settings, neither the validity of target specification nor the practical interpretation of the distance metric has been sufficiently addressed. Furthermore, most existing CE methods focus on altering predictions rather than optimizing a decision objective, even though real-world decision-making often requires explicit objective maximization. To address these limitations, we formulate CE as a profit maximization problem in management and marketing contexts and propose a framework termed profit-based counterfactual explanation (PBCE).
